tree
6b042d773121e3125cf73245ec7b31a73f44f3ec
parent
a9499715ac4e4419c303961bebe781517f80daca [ diff ] 
page_action: Pass ui::ColorId directly in AnchoredMessageBubbleView

Pass `ui::kColorSysOnSurface` and `ui::kColorSysOnSurfaceSubtle`
directly when configuring `label_`, `close_button_`, and `menu_button_`
in `AnchoredMessageBubbleView`'s constructor instead of resolving them
on every theme change. `views::Label`, `views::ImageButton`, and
`views::MenuButton` resolve `ui::ColorId` automatically on theme
changes, making `AnchoredMessageBubbleView::OnThemeChanged()` redundant.

Screenshot: https://screenshot.googleplex.com/ZDhxLL5puJo3NNj
Bug: 394420459

bookmarks: Remove redundant OnThemeChanged() from SavedTabGroupButton

UpdateButtonLayout() already configures the button's text, background,
border, and icon using ui::ColorId values during construction and data
updates. Remove OnThemeChanged() so LabelButton handles theme updates.

performance_controls: Pass ColorId in PerformanceInterventionButton

of resolving it via GetColorProvider() in OnThemeChanged(). Call

OnThemeChanged(), as ToolbarButton updates the icon automatically on

51518197d078bbe1b323e0c85c8f9dbf7e263a3c
parent
05364396ca951d53f9add5c7b901098ccb62bd3b [ diff ] 
[PM] Merge SiteDataCacheInspector into SiteDataCache

Both SiteDataCache implementations also implemented
SiteDataCacheInspector and registered themselves in a second
SiteDataCacheFactory map that always mirrored the cache map. Move the
inspector methods into SiteDataCache and remove the inspector map and
the self-registration. As a result, the caches no longer need to know
their browser context ID.

tree
948f9818e8a7507a53a6ef57f5d20f2f3935c256
parent
9bf6b9a7f0644264c9fdaa04b440cae445b24b1a [ diff ] 
Validate XMLHttpRequest and EventSource state after synchronous events

Synchronous event listeners invoked during XMLHttpRequest readystatechange
or upload progress dispatch can abort or reopen the request, changing
state_ or loader_. Capture the current loader before dispatching events
in TrackProgress(), DidFinishLoading(), DidSendData(), DidReceiveData(),
and DidDownloadData(), and return early if error_ is set or the state
or loader has changed.

Similarly, stop EventSourceParser in EventSource::NetworkRequestEnded()
and return immediately from EventSourceParser::AddBytes() if ParseLine()
stops the parser.

Bug: 574804521

3d389d09ba44087686770c8d36d32ed5191282fa [ diff ] 
Prefetch: Support custom loader timeout for prefetch ahead of imminent navigation

`kPrefetchAheadOfImminentNavigation` disables the
`PrefetchStreamingURLLoader` timeout by default so in-flight prefetches
are not cancelled while a navigation is waiting on them, whereas
`use_prefetch_loader_timeout` reverts to the standard speculative
prefetch timeout.

Adds a `TimeDelta` parameter `prefetch_loader_timeout` to
`kPrefetchAheadOfImminentNavigation` (default `0s`, preserving existing
behavior unless configured) so an independent timeout threshold can be
configured specifically for prefetches ahead of imminent navigations.
When positive, `prefetch_loader_timeout` takes precedence over
`use_prefetch_loader_timeout`.

No behavioral change by default.

TAG=agy
CONV=6fa76aab-c730-4ece-8983-cd148d9decc7

fb3544b50153d9e223610417a2e70298cb6e0061

d33891e126d23f3f34c215b422049af83168e193 [ diff ] 
Prefetch: Add BlockUntilHeadDuration2 and dual-log legacy

`Prefetch.BlockUntilHeadDuration.PerMatchingCandidate.*` uses
`base::UmaHistogramTimes()`, which caps its upper bucket at 10 seconds.
When `BlockUntilHead` or loader timeouts are relaxed or disabled, block
durations can exceed 10 seconds and saturate the overflow bucket.

Adds extended-range (1ms to 3 minutes, 50 buckets via
`base::UmaHistogramMediumTimes()`) histograms
`Prefetch.BlockUntilHeadDuration2.PerMatchingCandidate.*` while
temporarily dual-logging and deprecating the legacy
`Prefetch.BlockUntilHeadDuration.PerMatchingCandidate.*` histograms
until server-side configs migrate.

Test: content_unittests --gtest_filter=*BlockUntilHead*

parent
4de1f61fb16e483a35f6f5c6e72a32eb50ddd65f [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Lazily create IconLabelBubbleView separator layer

IconLabelBubbleView::SeparatorView previously called SetPaintToLayer()
unconditionally in its constructor. Because LocationBarView holds ~50
IconLabelBubbleView instances (PageActionView, ContentSettingImageView,
LocationIconView, SelectedKeywordView) and nearly all of them never show
a separator, every Chrome browser and web app window allocated ~48-51
unused hidden ui::Layer and cc::PictureLayer objects (~42-52% of all
layers in BrowserWidget).

Create the separator layer lazily in SeparatorView::UpdateOpacity() only
when the separator is visible.

Interleaved A/B ui.WindowCyclePerf on octopus (Celeron N4000, 14 samples
per variant):
- Enter.PresentationTime: -11.7% (2w), -5.5% (8w), -6.0% (16w)
- AnimationSmoothness.Show.16windows: 87.3% -> 100.0% (+12.7pp)
- AnimationSmoothness.Container.8windows: 93.9% -> 97.7% (+3.8pp)
- AnimationSmoothness.Container.16windows: 76.0% -> 85.2% (+9.2pp)
- 16-window compositor frame time: DoUpdateLayers -21.0%,
  PullProperties -24.9%, UpdateDrawProperties -28.1%

Bug: b:577853808
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=IconLabelBubbleViewTest.*
Test: interactive_ui_tests --gtest_filter=*SelectedKeywordView*
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=LocationBarViewBrowserTest.*

9cd81ead6728963ea816c6027bf0804375c01817 [ diff ] 
Replace Debug build with V8 DCHECK build in security-for-agents

security-for-agents.md previously asked for full Debug builds
(is_debug=true) only to trigger V8 DCHECK failures in chrome or d8.
Automated reproduction does not build is_debug=true because it is slow,
memory-heavy, and trips non-V8 DCHECKs.

Replace Debug with a DCHECK build (Release plus
v8_dcheck_always_on=true) and state that is_debug=true is unsupported.
Update vuln-report-check to accept "dcheck" (manual until automated
support lands) and reject "debug" with guidance pointing to "dcheck".

Linux wrapper: Drop `exec -a "$0"` to avoid chaining `cat` on restart
📋 Metadata
commit
2cfa704a3a35197acf593ffbc7fd054ac2f23339
[ log ]
author
Thomas Anderson <thomasanderson@chromium.org>
Sat Nov 28 02:09:38 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Nov 28 02:09:38 2026
tree
bca40e363517d0980cb55b740ffc36ab3e3220f2
parent
54d6157bf8712af52466deda4847428c6ea7a62d [ diff ] 
Linux wrapper: Drop `exec -a "$0"` to avoid chaining `cat` on restart

The Linux launcher wrapper used `exec -a "$0"` to pass the wrapper path
as argv[0] to Chrome. In-browser restarts (e.g. chrome://restart,
about:flags, and post-update relaunches) re-exec argv[0], which re-ran
the wrapper script on every restart and chained two additional `cat`
processes for stdout/stderr sanitization each time.

Drop `-a "$0"` so Chrome receives the real binary path as argv[0] and
self-restarts exec the binary directly, inheriting the already-sanitized
std{in,out,err} descriptors and wrapper environment variables
(CHROME_WRAPPER, CHROME_VERSION_EXTRA, GNOME_DISABLE_CRASH_DIALOG).
Add a comment noting that self-restarts bypass the wrapper, and add a
unit test in installer_unittest.py verifying argv[0], CHROME_WRAPPER,
and stdout/stderr pipe sanitization.

Fixed: 40555961

[iOS] Update zero-state detent height on keyboard dismiss

Observe keyboard dismissal in the Gemini container and recalculate the
zero-state medium detent height once the keyboard finishes hiding, so
the sheet reflects any content height changes that occurred while the
keyboard was visible.

[v8] Add V8ShrinkStringTable fieldtrial and WebView flag

V8's off-heap StringTable retains oversized backing buffers after
Mark-Compact sweeps dead internalized strings when no subsequent JS
string internalization runs (such as in an idle kept-alive Android
WebView renderer). V8 added an off-by-default --shrink-string-table
flag to reclaim this memory at Mark-Compact safepoints.

Prepare for cross-platform Finch evaluation of V8ShrinkStringTable:

- Allowlist V8Flag_shrink_string_table in Android WebView's
  ProductionSupportedFlagList so WebView forwards the feature state
  through gin's V8FeatureVisitor.
- Add the V8ShrinkStringTable study entry to
  fieldtrial_testing_config.json across desktop and mobile platforms.

TAG=agy
CONV=c1397e04-10a0-4ab3-a64b-6faf615fd34d

tree
4e66dab52cd28184e0e6d58132a5e497031dc156
parent
1f9ba907b65d348e2aab7fdc079b4ffe7e491120 [ diff ] 
Prerender: Start pending prerenders asynchronously on visibility change

`OnVisibilityChanged()` started the pending prerender synchronously
while `WebContents` was notifying the visibility change. Starting a
navigation can synchronously run arbitrary code, which can destroy
objects that the code changing the visibility still uses.

Post a non-nestable task to start it instead. The task checks the state
again when it runs, and repeated visibility changes post at most one
task.

Also remove the `CHECK` in `CreateAndStartHost()`. With the deferred
start, `CreateAndStartHost()` can find an older pending host at the head
of the queue.

TAG=agy
CONV=41c72d34-bebb-47e0-af99-2c85d1462983

is chronically flaking across Windows CI and CQ builders (1556 flaky +

verdicts across 619 CLs in 72h, plus 39-50/50 builds across M156-M159
Win10 Tests x64), failing with TabWasHiddenLaterShown instead of
TabWasHiddenStaysHidden.

[Indigo] Don't reset or toast another page from a non-primary manager

IndigoImageReplacementManager went through the controller, which acts
on the primary page, so a disconnect or generate error on a page that
was no longer primary reset and toasted the new page, and a late
success showed the toolbar there. Reset such a page locally, record
results without a toast, and skip tab-level UI for it.

TAG=agy

f4935cf250efa3a7715fa9f5b0f270b31589cb39 [ diff ] 
[NTP Customization Sync] Set the daily refresh timestamp on persist

Before this CL, if a synced daily refresh theme arrived and Chrome was
killed before an NTP was shown, the next startup treated daily refresh
as already due. A synced Chrome color rotated immediately instead of
waiting a day.

Synced themes are saved to SharedPreferences as soon as they arrive,
which clears the previous theme's data including its timestamp. However,
the new daily refresh timestamp was only written later when the theme
was applied on an NTP.

Record the daily refresh timestamp when persisting a theme collection or
Chrome color, so it is always saved alongside the daily refresh setting.

[Zucchini] Add Zucchini-info: Command to inspect supported architecture versions

Zucchini's patch format tracks a global major/minor version alongside
per-architecture disassembler versions. Previously, given a compiled
Zucchini binary, there was no way to inspect which architectures and
disassembler versions it supports. Upcoming AArch64 ELF patch-size
optimizations will introduce a version-2 AArch64 disassembler alongside
legacy v1, making binary-level capability inspection essential for
debugging, scripting, and rollout tooling.

This CL adds the `-info` command (`Zucchini-info`) to print the Zucchini
format version and a formatted per-architecture table of default, `gen`,
and `apply` versions (currently identical single versions per
architecture, with separate columns preparing for upcoming multi-version
support).

Details:
* disassembler_{dex,ztf}.{h,cc}:
  * Add `kExeTypeString` constants on `DisassemblerDex` and
    `DisassemblerZtf` (matching Win32/ELF traits) and reuse them in
    `GetExeTypeString()` so architecture display names have a single
    source of truth.
* element_detection.{h,cc}:
  * Add `DisassemblerVersionInfo` and `GetSupportedDisassemblers()`
    returning a `base::span` over a static table of compiled-in
    executable types, display names, and versions.
  * Update `DisassemblerVersionOfType()` to look up versions from
    `GetSupportedDisassemblers()` to avoid duplicating version lists.
* patch_{reader,writer}.cc:
  * Reject `kInvalidVersion` explicitly in `ParseElementMatch()` and
    `SerializeElementMatch()` so unlisted or disabled architectures
    cannot silently emit or match `kInvalidVersion` headers.
* zucchini_tools.{h,cc}, zucchini_commands.{h,cc}, main_utils.cc:
  * Add `PrintInfo()`, `MainInfo()`, and the zero-argument `-info`
    command.
* README.md: Document `zucchini -info`.

Testing:
* element_detection_unittest.cc: Add
  `ElementDetectionVersionTest.GetSupportedDisassemblers` verifying
  compiled-in types and `DisassemblerVersionOfType()` against each
  disassembler's `kVersion` (and `kInvalidVersion` when disabled).
* patch_read_write_unittest.cc: Add
  `PatchTest.SerializeElementMatchUnsupportedExe`.
* zucchini_tools_unittest.cc (new, registered in BUILD.gn): Add
  `ZucchiniToolsTest.PrintInfo` verifying header lines, column
  alignment (`max_name_len` padding), and formatted version output.

eecdb8e20da6bf63eefff2e68dcb46a4d8bfd71a [ diff ] 
[downloads] Move warning survey calls out of DownloadsDOMHandler

Move desktop-only HaTS (download_warning_desktop_hats_utils.h) and
TrustSafetySentimentService calls out of DownloadsDOMHandler into
downloads_ui_platform_handlers.{h,_desktop.cc}, using three small enums
in the platform header to keep it include-free and Android-buildable.

No behavior change for reachable inputs, with two minor differences:
- CanShowDownloadWarningHatsSurvey() is now an early return in the
  desktop helper instead of a CHECK at the three action sites (already
  guaranteed by callers).
- A null main download manager returns early instead of dereferencing.

The browser_shutdown::HasShutdownStarted() guard stays first in
OnDownloadsPageDismissed(). Adds two unit tests covering the HaTS heed
and page-dismissal paths.

0a7fe1e43438804cd6f4a878e1f8b024de8a3328
parent
89c7d1357dfbfea3bb76156bda5c1196cbf94790 [ diff ] 
[gpu] Split write and read SyncTokens in SharedImageExportResult

Previously, `SharedImageExportResult` stored all SyncTokens in a single
`sync_tokens_` vector, and `ClientSharedImage::EndExport()` recorded all
returned tokens in `read_sync_token_map_` as read-only under
`UseAutomaticSyncTokenManagement`. When an importer writes to an
imported SharedImage (e.g. WebXR), this left the owner's
`write_sync_token_` stale instead of tracking the latest write, which
would cause subsequent read-only accesses (that only wait on
`write_sync_token_`) to miss waiting on the importer's write.

This CL splits `SharedImageExportResult` into write and read SyncTokens:

- Replace `sync_tokens_` in `SharedImageExportResult` (and its Mojom
  struct/traits) with `write_sync_token` and `read_sync_tokens`.
- Add `was_write_sync_token_updated_` to `ClientSharedImage` (kept false
  upon import from `ExportedSharedImage`) so `EndImport()` only returns
  a non-empty `write_sync_token` when a write occurred after import.
- Update `EndExport()` and `EndExportAsVector()` in automatic mode to
  store a non-empty `write_sync_token` as a write (clearing prior read
  SyncTokens) before storing the returned `read_sync_tokens`.

No functional change when `UseAutomaticSyncTokenManagement` is disabled.

tree
ed2f8679746b5c56015cc68b83d6c2df9e678155
parent
3ce178b971b5deab237c121ea503fc0f4154a91d [ diff ] 
[ReadAloud] Block entrypoint on non-article web apps

Legacy Read Aloud relies on Speakr's CheckURL RPC to filter unsupported
URLs before offering playback, suppressing sites that are unsuitable
(non-article sites and opt-out domains). Without equivalent filtering in
Native Read Aloud, all valid HTTP(S) URLs are treated as distillable.

This change introduces ReadAloudBlocklist with a built-in domain
blocklist (captures core domains in reference blocklist in CheckURL,
matches via normalized url::Origin host, strips "www." and "m." for
behavioral parity) and wires it into
ReadAloudService::CheckReadability() and Initialize() to suppress the
entrypoint and reject playback on blocked domains. The blocklist is
gated by the kReadAloudBlockedDomains kill-switch and will serve as the
offline fallback for the planned Component Updater integration (dynamic
blocklist).

parent
3a9c9949cb06d34aab3071e975437b27fe11f772 [ diff ] 
[NTP Customization Sync] Update history entry on daily refresh rotation

Before this CL, after a daily refresh rotated to a new image, the theme
history entry still showed the old image instead of the current one.
Selecting it also switched back to the old image. A daily Chrome color
already updates its history entry on rotation (crrev.com/c/8712683).

When NTP theme sync is enabled, moving the new image into
theme_collection_images/ now also updates the collection's local history
entry to the new image. The old image file is deleted if nothing else
uses it. When sync is disabled, there is no history and nothing changes.

Known limitations:
- At startup, the history entry still shows the old image for a brief
  window (typically a few seconds) until deferred startup finishes
  moving the new file.
- If the active collection is not in local history (for example, if
  Chrome was killed before the customization sheet closed), its old
  image file is not deleted on rotation. A follow-up CL will handle
  this.
- An old ntp_background_image file left over from before sync was
  enabled is not deleted. Impact is low because it is at most one file
  and does not grow across rotations.

Demo:
https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NjIyOTE1MjkxODE0Mjk3NnxiMDMxNTM3My1iNw

49b9a6f032424190e0a0cf3d4efe1f1ae7f6e7b7
[ log ]
author

tree
ada0567bd02358720b29af7e04ef793b403490eb
parent
828fd1b14efc4ab201966bcc36c5cc6bf119afbf [ diff ] 
[X11] Prefer text/plain;charset=utf-8 over text/plain for text

Drag sources such as Firefox and Zotero offer text/plain and
text/plain;charset=utf-8 (but not UTF8_STRING), and put UTF-8 data in
both. GetTextAtomsFrom() listed text/plain first, and
SelectionData::GetText() decodes text/plain as Latin-1, so non-ASCII
dropped text was mangled (e.g. "ñ" became "Ã±").

Move text/plain;charset=utf-8 right after UTF8_STRING so explicitly
UTF-8 types are preferred over the Latin-1 ones.

Fixed: 41342086

3c09a8da9bb68e00d13ba4fc316e77ced1a8d17c
parent
8c1874c443cb9533abcca5bdaa933e7cd10277ce [ diff ] 
actor: Reset per-tool-call state in ProcessNextFromDispatcher

Both `executing_request_ and external_tool_failure_reason_ are scoped to
a single ToolRequest so should be cleared whenever one ends. Doing so
from ProcessNextFromDispatcher is a good place to do it because this is
the mechanism through which we start every tool call as well as ending
the current dispatcher.

42aa46b6b8eae3a39cee78a0e331a3bfbbf26cd8 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Fix and re-enable testAllowDinosaurEasterEggDisabled on device

The disabled dino page only reacts to a keydown event. On physical
devices the simulated hardware key press did not reach the page, so the
test timed out waiting for the disabled message.

Dispatch the Space keydown from JavaScript in the page instead, wait for
the snackbar element before dispatching, and re-enable the test on
device.

This change was written with AI assistance.

Cq-Include-Trybots: chrome/try:iphone-device,ipad-device

09903a1046f6dd3f108ae0243b5f4232292647a8
parent
41a31bd84f97d9b7aed6b4fc064d2a33c41af5e2 [ diff ] 
[Perf] Unregister WebContentsObserver in PwaBottomSheetController

Previously, SheetState.HIDDEN cleared mWebContentsObserver without
calling observe(null), and hide and early-return paths retained
mWebContents and UI references on the window-scoped controller.

Consolidate PwaBottomSheetController teardown into destroy() so
SheetState.HIDDEN and requestBottomSheetInstaller() early-return paths
all unregister mWebContentsObserver via observe(null) and clear
mWebContents, mBottomSheetController, mPwaBottomSheetContent, mModel,
mScreenshotAdapter, and mNativePwaBottomSheetController. Guard
addWebAppScreenshot(Bitmap) when mScreenshotAdapter is null, and add
unit tests in PwaBottomSheetControllerTest covering dismissal,
requestShowContent failure, null BottomSheetController early return,
and post-cleanup screenshot callbacks.

4a34b9eba809339c17c0605bdf7e675c69e5e3eb
[ log ]
author

Fri Nov 27 21:36:21 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 27 21:36:21 2026
tree
6bc9f6eef63a77bd6ea92822cb0ecf6bc59d2ede
parent
ee5bdf1c3c42fe942acbca60b4d4b3c315e4d108 [ diff ] 
[SuperSize] Strip compiler suffixes from switch table symbols

LLVM appends module-counter, promotion, and relocation suffixes such as
(.8), (.rel), or .llvm.<hash> to .Lswitch.table.* symbols. When
unrelated codegen or ThinLTO changes shift those suffixes, SuperSize
matched shifted names against different switch tables and reported
spurious symbol size deltas even when no switch table changed size. Also
enable demangling for Rust .Lswitch.table._R* switch table symbols.

Add playbook for reducing .data.rel.ro and native relocations

Existing relocation docs explain why .data.rel.ro hurts startup latency
and dirty RAM, but do not catalog concrete patterns for removing
relocations without regressing 32-bit binary size. Adding a playbook
gives authors a single reference for choosing and verifying .rodata
representations.

tree
2c5a915b2c231d9bef16eafd046dee3c94000910
parent
5a7ab56b2ce8db31784fa4681a38850e138c5a42 [ diff ] 
Defer CloudProfileReportingService scheduler creation to BEST_EFFORT

Unconditionally observing CloudPolicyCore in Init() caused
OnCoreConnected() to run synchronously during startup before
BEST_EFFORT UI tasks are enabled, moving UploadReportOnProfileOpen
uploads into the initial DMServer startup burst alongside policy
fetches.

Keep CloudPolicyCore observation active across account switches, but
schedule scheduler creation on BEST_EFFORT from both Init() and
OnCoreConnected() while still tearing down stale mismatched clients
synchronously on reconnect.

actor: Convert AttemptLogin to use a custom dispatcher

Convert AttemptLoginTool into the first "composite tool". The
ToolRequest creates a dispatcher which first dispatches an internal
version of the AttemptLoginToolRequest which invokes the
AttemptLoginTool. If that succeeds the dispatcher sends a
ClickToolRequest.

This sequence was previously achieved with EnqueueFollowup which is now
removed.

The result-merging helpers are moved from execution_engine.cc into
tool_result.{h,cc} so they can be shared. The end state should be that
ExecutionEngine doesn't need to merge results, only composite tools.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
[PM] Add option for FrameAudibleVoter to vote USER_VISIBLE
📋 Metadata
commit
e2ee78c873f8758e74b86e63341e70dd2bf0f886
[ log ]
author
Etienne Pierre-doray <etiennep@chromium.org>
Fri Nov 27 21:23:53 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 27 21:23:53 2026
tree
730be9a9ee1e75d62b994dc47acf8e120019bffe
parent
83a143773881ad94cfb1569e67c2b2eb48f8d78f [ diff ] 
[PM] Add option for FrameAudibleVoter to vote USER_VISIBLE

Adds the "audible_frame_user_visible_priority" param (default false) to
kPMProcessPriorityPolicy. When true, audible frames vote for
base::Process::Priority::kUserVisible instead of kUserBlocking.

With UserVisibleProcessPriority disabled (default), kUserVisible is
remapped to kUserBlocking at the OS level in
RenderProcessHostImpl::UpdateProcessPriority(), so this only affects
renderer-side consumers of the process priority (isolate priority with
IsolatesPriorityUseProcessPriority, RestrictThreadPoolInBackground).
The audio rendering thread is realtime regardless of process priority.

This lets hidden audible renderers keep v8's memory-saving GC behaviour
(e.g. combined with IsolatesPriorityUserVisibleAsBestEffort) without
lowering their OS priority.

Bug: 40942028

tree
5bef5c049200937f86471f0f95c83b4c1f9493f3
parent
b8924c1532f95340fb55646fb11205c65baa7738 [ diff ] 
[Multiprofile] Add PreNativeProfileKeyedMap

Some objects are created before native is initialized, when there is no
Profile yet, but belong to a profile. With multiple profiles, such an
object must end up with the profile it was set up for.

PreNativeProfileKeyedMap wraps ProfileKeyedMap.
1. The map takes the factory in its constructor, like the destroy
   action. Before native is initialized, getForProfile(null) returns a
   single pre-native instance, created by the factory with a null
   profile, for the profile Chrome is expected to start with, whose base
   name (ProfileManager.getLastUsedProfileBaseName()) is read when the
   pre-native instance is created.
2. The pre-native instance is moved to that profile when the profile, or
   one of its off-the-record profiles, is first requested, or when it is
   the first regular profile added. Profiles are matched by comparing
   Profile.getBaseName() of the original profile with the recorded base
   name.
3. If the first regular profile added is another one, or no base name
   was recorded, the pre-native instance is destroyed, and the profile
   gets a new object when it is requested.
4. Once the first profile is added, callers must pass a profile. Objects
   are destroyed with their profile, as in ProfileKeyedMap, or when the
   map is destroyed.

Follow-up CLs will use the map for objects that are created before
native.

Test: chrome_junit_tests -f '*PreNativeProfileKeyedMapTest*'

tree
166f27fa422f8e3e2a98d6e7b77ee2503e6fc893
parent
e450dac73fb8394f2ebefc5487b799d379cba7dc [ diff ] 
[css] Snapshot cross-fade clients before notifying ImageChanged

When a cross-fade's underlying image fails (e.g. via window.stop()),
CSSCrossfadeValue::ObserverProxy forwards ImageChanged to every client.
A client <img> using the cross-fade as generated content then switches
to fallback content, which creates its UA shadow root and synchronously
detaches its children's layout objects. Those children are themselves
clients of the same cross-fade, so CSSImageGeneratorValue::clients_ is
modified while being iterated.

Iterate over a snapshot of the clients instead, and skip clients that
were removed during dispatch.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
[TabModel] Run resumeSaveTabList callbacks when the save is cancelled
📋 Metadata
commit
5a8bc6938a04729fa8b3b0837e46e9cae175275b
[ log ]
author
Calder Kitagawa <ckitagawa@chromium.org>
Fri Nov 27 20:45:17 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 27 20:45:17 2026
tree
e789a2e4e2f90023544a38c42c09433d0528694f
parent
99aa4e6c25f1f88e923822c5f7f0556911a164b6 [ diff ] 
[TabModel] Run resumeSaveTabList callbacks when the save is cancelled

Moving a tab group to another window pauses tab list saving, detaches
the tabs, and then calls TabPersistentStore#resumeSaveTabList() with a
callback that launches the target window. The callback waited for the
pending tab list save to finish. If that save was cancelled first, the
callback never ran, so the detached tabs were never handed to the target
window. The save is cancelled when the store is destroyed, for example
because the source window closed once it became empty, and when
saveState() replaces it with a synchronous save as the window stops.

Keep the pending callbacks in the store. On destroy(), let an in-flight
SaveListTask finish writing its already-extracted metadata and run the
callbacks from onPostExecute() (skipping observer notifications after
teardown), so moved tabs are removed from the source metadata file
before callbacks attach them elsewhere; calls to resumeSaveTabList()
after destroy() still wait on an in-flight SaveListTask if one exists
and post directly otherwise. In saveState(), run the callbacks after the
StrictMode finally block once the synchronous metadata and tab state
writes complete.

Bug: b:586912677

[Autofill] Remove unnecessary set_frame_token() calls in tests

Now that TestAutofillDriver initializes its frame token to a unique,
non-empty LocalFrameToken by default, tests no longer need to call
`set_frame_token(test::MakeLocalFrameToken())` just to get a valid
distinct token.

This CL removes those redundant `set_frame_token()` calls.

Reland "Make AppliedTextDecoration equality independent of :visited"

This is a reland of commit 5cf568039baef934b5d67d4d0bbc34b15e240934.

crrev.com/c/8734802 landed after CQ+1 and added a per-element DOMNodeId
source_id to PrecomputedHoverState, causing the test equality check
between two distinct <a> elements to fail. Update the test to compare
PrecomputedHoverState presence, is_current_visible, and IsInEffect().

Original change's description:
> Make AppliedTextDecoration equality independent of :visited
>
> TextDecorationOf() stored the color picked by VisitedDependentColor()
> in AppliedTextDecoration, and operator== compared it. Visited and
> unvisited links therefore produced different style diffs and
> different decoration run merging, which is observable and leaks
> history. Store both the unvisited and visited colors plus a bit
> saying which one to paint, and compare only the two colors.
> PrecomputedHoverChangesTextDecoration() had the same dependency and
> now compares both colors as well.
>
> Fixed: 578663704

Fixed: 578663704

1853c97e891cf5923b5a65fcd8fafec0cf21ce44 [ diff ] 
Drop intents received during startup

If we receive an intent during startup we get an inconsistent view of
what surface or webpage we're launching into since startup is async.

Drop any intents sent during startup - any intents received this way are
most likely either malicious or programmatic bugs (apps that double-fire
intents).

6e0370df085516b50d2ce860cacf0e1b4058feb1
parent
3836d0c2c54b5f9ec84365f653ab4470388e9b03 [ diff ] 
Restore gating for adding AccessibilityNodeInfo.MathInfo

This CL restores the AccessibilityAndroidMath feature flag gating
for adding AccessibilityNodeInfo.MathInfo

With this CL, AccessibilityNodeInfo.MathInfo is added whenever
the AccessibilityAndroidMath feature flag is on but
irrespective of the Android Talkback version.

The rationale is to enable disabling the feature using
solely the Chrome feature. This avoids having to synchronize
disabling the feature via Chrome variations and via Talkback
server flags. The AccessibilityNodeInfo.MathInfo code is not
gated on the Talkback version in order to increase
confidence that the AccessibilityAndroidMath feature won't
cause any regressions.

When the feature is enabled in Talkback, but disabled in
Chrome the current code leaves the user in a broken state.

Bug: 586563165

tree
77ad57957e8497f329a3bd78d80778fa1aa3ef12
parent
cdb2ce7ab2bad14770380be2253c0aee00da7bb8 [ diff ] 
[X11] Use per-monitor work areas from _GTK_WORKAREAS_D<n>

_NET_WORKAREA is a single rectangle for the whole screen, so on
multi-monitor setups Chrome applied it to at most one display, and
panels on other monitors (or on an edge shared between monitors) were
ignored. window.screen.avail* then reported the full monitor bounds.

Mutter-based window managers (GNOME Shell, Cinnamon's Muffin) also set
_GTK_WORKAREAS_D<desktop>, a list of per-monitor work areas, and
advertise it with _GTK_WORKAREAS in _NET_SUPPORTED. When available,
intersect each display's bounds with those rectangles, as GTK does in
gdk_x11_monitor_get_workarea(), and fall back to _NET_WORKAREA
otherwise. ConvertDisplayBoundsToDips() now keeps work area insets,
and the display list is refreshed when the property or the current
desktop changes.

[PM] Replace FrameNode::IsIntersectingLargeArea with 3-state enum
📋 Metadata
commit
1542d339b4737c60831d85cbe331187a5601d67c
[ log ]
author
Joe Mason <joenotcharles@google.com>
Fri Nov 27 19:58:30 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 27 19:58:30 2026
tree
7d4a01a4acfe9eadf6ed6cdbb675d05554b51974
parent
7c11cb5ecfebc76500a235dac318c78875b52238 [ diff ] 
[PM] Replace FrameNode::IsIntersectingLargeArea with 3-state enum

FrameNode::IsIntersectingLargeArea() was a boolean defaulting to true to
encode ImportantFrameDecorator's "unknown -> important" fallback policy
directly in the graph node. Its value was overridden when the viewport
intersection was kNotIntersecting, making the exact relationship between
various ViewportIntersection states and the "intersecting large area"
concept hard to reason about.

Replace IsIntersectingLargeArea(), SetIsIntersectingLargeArea(), and
OnIsIntersectingLargeAreaChanged() with ViewportIntersectionArea
({kUnknown, kSmallOrNone, kLarge}) via GetViewportIntersectionArea() and
OnViewportIntersectionAreaChanged():

- Outermost main frames return kLarge unless overridden. (Because they
  are assumed to intersect the entire viewport.
  `is_intersecting_large_area_` was not updated for these frames so
  stayed at the default true.)

- Clamped to kUnknown while GetViewportIntersection() is kUnknown, and
  to kSmallOrNone while not kIntersecting.
  (`is_intersecting_large_area_` used the default true for kUnknown, and
  clamped to false when not kIntersecting.)

- Same-process child frames inherit their parent's area before
  OnViewportIntersectionChanged() fires so observers never see torn
  state, while ImportantFrameDecorator explicitly handles kUnknown ->
  true.

Also replaces SetIsIntersectingLargeAreaImpl() with
SetViewportIntersectionAndArea(), which is called whenever either the
ViewportIntersection or ViewportIntersectionArea changes. This ensures
the correct observers are notified whenever the caller-visible result of
GetViewportIntersection() or GetViewportIntersectionArea() changes.

Factors out checks used when updating ViewportIntersection to helper
functions to ensure ViewportIntersection and ViewportIntersectionArea
are treated consistently.

Bug: None
Test: components_unittests (*FrameNode*, *Decorator*, *Priority*, *PageNode*)
Test: components_browsertests (*PerformanceManager*)
Test: browser_tests (*FrameNodeImplBrowserTest*)

[partition_alloc] Enable user space zero segment on ASan builds

52b76b537ce0f14f02198bf4c8555d8722d342bd
parent
b46a218087a0871f6994264a7aa9b3e3ea535659 [ diff ] 
[partition_alloc] Enable user space zero segment on ASan builds

Previously, `enable_user_space_zero_segment` required
`use_partition_alloc_as_malloc`, leaving the first 4 GiB + 1 MiB
unprotected on ASan builds. On 64-bit Linux x64 ASan, `LowMem`
is unmapped and `LowShadow` is mapped `PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE`, so
uninstrumented V8 JIT/Wasm accesses to low addresses can write into
`LowShadow` instead of faulting and trigger false-positive V8 sandbox
violation reports.

This CL enables the user space zero segment on 64-bit Linux, ChromeOS,
and Android ASan builds:
It achieves this by running `InitZeroSegmentForAsan()` via a priority-101
constructor when `!USE_PARTITION_ALLOC_AS_MALLOC` which runs immediately
after ASan's `.preinit_array` setup.
The constructor reserves `LowMem` as `PROT_NONE` before modifying
`LowShadow` to PROT_READ, and bailing out gracefully if pre-existing
low mappings (e.g., non-PIE binaries or non-Native-Zygote Android
ART) or `ASAN_OPTIONS=protect_shadow_gap=0` are present.

Bug: 585451337

2f861f39226ffad353f64a6fb21515b75cefc58e

6540fbf5e3e6201e5b67dd17c8930f015f25ba4b [ diff ] 
[Perf] Remove back stack listeners in SettingsPageFragmentDelegateImpl

SettingsPageFragmentDelegateImpl registered fresh
this::updateBackPressState method references on the host and
multi-column child FragmentManagers without keeping references to
remove them in destroySettings(). Store a single
OnBackStackChangedListener instance and the registered host and
multi-column child FragmentManagers, register via
getHostFragmentManager() and getChildFragmentManagerOrNull(), and
unregister the listener in destroySettings().

Bug: b:591056895
